> AGENT

Now I need to understand the full picture. The cursor[bot] comment claims that when `condenseAndUpdateState` returns `false` (condensation failed), the code falls through to line 621 and calls `updateBaseCommitIfChanged`, which updates `BaseCommit` to the new HEAD. This would make the old shadow branch (named by old BaseCommit) inaccessible.

Let me verify this is actually a real issue by thinking through the logic carefully:

1. Line 584-589: `ActionCondense` case - if `condenseAndUpdateState` returns `false`, `condensed` stays `false`
2. Line 621: `if !condensed && !hasPendingMigration` → `true`, so `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` is called
3. This updates `state.BaseCommit` to `newHead`
4. But the shadow branch is still named with the **old** BaseCommit hash
5. Next time the strategy looks for the shadow branch, it will use the **new** BaseCommit, and won't find the old branch

This is a legitimate bug. The shadow branch still has the data, but the session state points to a non-existent branch name. Let me also check what actions lead to `ActionCondense` to understand which phases are affected:

Here's a summary of the one unaddressed review comment on PR #172:

## Thread: cursor[bot] — Condensation failure orphans shadow branch (Medium Severity)

**File:** `manual_commit_hooks.go:621-623`

**Claim:** When `condenseAndUpdateState()` returns `false` (condensation failed), `condensed` stays `false` and `hasPendingMigration` stays `false`, so line 621 (`if !condensed && !hasPendingMigration`) evaluates true and calls `updateBaseCommitIfChanged()`. This updates `state.BaseCommit` to the new HEAD, but the shadow branch is still named with the **old** BaseCommit hash. The session state now points to a non-existent shadow branch, making the data inaccessible.

**My analysis: This is a legitimate bug.**

The logic flow on condensation failure:
1. `ActionCondense` case (line 585-589): `condenseAndUpdateState` returns `false` → `condensed` stays `false`
2. Line 621: `!condensed && !hasPendingMigration` → `true` 
3. `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` updates `state.BaseCommit` to `newHead`
4. Line 626: State is saved with the new BaseCommit
5. **Result:** Shadow branch `entire/<old-hash>-<worktreeHash>` still exists with the data, but state now references `entire/<new-hash>-<worktreeHash>` which doesn't exist

**The fix:** On condensation failure, we should **not** update `BaseCommit`. We should only update it when condensation succeeds (which already happens inside `condenseAndUpdateState` at line 694) or when there's nothing to condense. The fallthrough at line 621 should skip sessions that attempted condensation but failed.

The simplest fix would be to track whether condensation was **attempted** (regardless of success) and skip the `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` call in that case:

```go
condensed := false
condensationAttempted := false
// ...
case session.ActionCondense:
    if hasNew {
        condensationAttempted = true
        if s.condenseAndUpdateState(...) {
            condensed = true
        }
    } else {
        s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
    }
// ...
if !condensed && !hasPendingMigration && !condensationAttempted {
    s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
}
```

Want me to implement the fix?

    21→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an
    22→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    23→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.
    24→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {

Perfect. Now I have all the information needed. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the EventGitCommit behavior across all phases.

## EventGitCommit State Machine - Complete Analysis

### Summary Table

| Current Phase | Condition | New Phase | Actions |
|---|---|---|---|
| **PhaseIdle** | rebase in progress | PhaseIdle | *(none)* |
| **PhaseIdle** | normal commit | PhaseIdle | `ActionCondense`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |
| **PhaseActive** | rebase in progress | PhaseActive | *(none)* |
| **PhaseActive** | normal commit | PhaseActiveCommitted | `ActionMigrateShadowBranch`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |
| **PhaseActiveCommitted** | rebase in progress | PhaseActiveCommitted | *(none)* |
| **PhaseActiveCommitted** | normal commit | PhaseActiveCommitted | `ActionMigrateShadowBranch`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |
| **PhaseEnded** | rebase in progress | PhaseEnded | *(none)* |
| **PhaseEnded** | files touched + commit | PhaseEnded | `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |
| **PhaseEnded** | no files touched + commit | PhaseEnded | `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |
| **Empty/"unknown" phase** | rebase in progress | PhaseIdle | *(none)* |
| **Empty/"unknown" phase** | normal commit | PhaseIdle | `ActionCondense`, `ActionUpdateLastInteraction` |

---

### TransitionContext Fields & Their Effects

**TransitionContext** is defined as:
```go
type TransitionContext struct {
	HasFilesTouched    bool // len(FilesTouched) > 0
	IsRebaseInProgress bool // .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ exists
}
```

#### IsRebaseInProgress
- **Effect**: When `true`, ALL EventGitCommit transitions across ALL phases become no-ops
  - Phase stays the same
  - No actions are produced (empty action list)
- **Used in**: All 4 phase transitions (Idle, Active, ActiveCommitted, Ended)
- **Pattern**: Checked first with `if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress { return ... }`
- **Rationale**: During git rebase, intermediate commits should not trigger session state changes or condensation; only the final rebase completion matters

#### HasFilesTouched
- **Effect**: ONLY affects PhaseEnded transitions with EventGitCommit
- **PhaseIdle**: Ignored (not checked)
- **PhaseActive**: Ignored (not checked)
- **PhaseActiveCommitted**: Ignored (not checked)
- **PhaseEnded**: Determines whether to use `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` or `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles`
  - If `true`: `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` (preserve session if work was done)
  - If `false`: `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` (clean up if no actual changes)

---

### Detailed Transitions by Phase

#### 1. PhaseIdle + EventGitCommit

**Source code** (lines 162-169):
```go
case EventGitCommit:
	if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseIdle}
	}
	return TransitionResult{
		NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
		Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
	}
```

**Behavior**:
- **If rebase in progress**: Stay Idle, no actions
- **Normal commit**: Stay Idle, condense session + update interaction time
- **Interpretation**: A commit from idle state (no agent active) is treated as user work, so condensation preserves it

---

#### 2. PhaseActive + EventGitCommit

**Source code** (lines 196-203):
```go
case EventGitCommit:
	if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActive}
	}
	return TransitionResult{
		NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
		Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
	}
```

**Behavior**:
- **If rebase in progress**: Stay Active, no actions
- **Normal commit**: Transition to ActiveCommitted, migrate shadow branch + update interaction
- **Interpretation**: Agent is mid-turn; commit ends the turn phase, moving to committed state. Shadow branch must follow HEAD to new commit.

---

#### 3. PhaseActiveCommitted + EventGitCommit

**Source code** (lines 232-239):
```go
case EventGitCommit:
	if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
	}
	return TransitionResult{
		NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
		Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
	}
```

**Behavior**:
- **If rebase in progress**: Stay ActiveCommitted, no actions
- **Normal commit**: Stay ActiveCommitted, migrate shadow branch + update interaction
- **Interpretation**: Multiple commits are allowed while in ActiveCommitted state (e.g., user making additional commits mid-session). Each commit triggers shadow branch migration to track the new HEAD.

---

#### 4. PhaseEnded + EventGitCommit

**Source code** (lines 265-278):
```go
case EventGitCommit:
	if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
		return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseEnded}
	}
	if ctx.HasFilesTouched {
		return TransitionResult{
			NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
		}
	}
	return TransitionResult{
		NewPhase: PhaseEnded,
		Actions:  []Action{ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
	}
```

**Behavior** - Three conditional branches:
1. **If rebase in progress**: Stay Ended, no actions
2. **If files touched + normal commit**: Stay Ended, condense if files exist + update interaction
3. **If no files touched + normal commit**: Stay Ended, discard if no files + update interaction

**Interpretation**: After a session ends, a commit triggers cleanup. If the session touched files (has work), preserve it with conditional condensation. If it didn't touch files (orphaned/stale session), discard it.

---

### Special Actions & Their Producers

#### ActionCondense
- **Produced by**: PhaseIdle + EventGitCommit (normal)
- **Meaning**: Unconditionally condense session data to permanent storage

#### ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched
- **Produced by**: PhaseEnded + EventGitCommit (normal, with files touched)
- **Meaning**: Condense only if FilesTouched is non-empty (conditional cleanup)

#### ActionDiscardIfNoFiles
- **Produced by**: PhaseEnded + EventGitCommit (normal, without files touched)
- **Meaning**: Discard orphaned session if FilesTouched is empty

#### ActionMigrateShadowBranch
- **Produced by**:
  - PhaseActive + EventGitCommit (normal)
  - PhaseActiveCommitted + EventGitCommit (normal, every commit)
- **Meaning**: Move shadow branch to the new HEAD commit hash (needed when base commit changes)

#### ActionUpdateLastInteraction
- **Produced by**: Every EventGitCommit transition except rebase cases
- **Meaning**: Update LastInteractionTime timestamp on the session state

#### ActionWarnStaleSession
- **NOT produced by EventGitCommit** (produced by EventSessionStart instead)

#### ActionClearEndedAt
- **NOT produced by EventGitCommit** (produced by EventTurnStart and EventSessionStart instead)

---

### Rebase Behavior (All Phases)

**Pattern**: All transitions with `IsRebaseInProgress: true` follow this rule:
```
EventGitCommit + IsRebaseInProgress=true → Stay in same phase, produce no actions
```

This applies uniformly to:
- PhaseIdle → PhaseIdle (no actions)
- PhaseActive → PhaseActive (no actions)
- PhaseActiveCommitted → PhaseActiveCommitted (no actions)
- PhaseEnded → PhaseEnded (no actions)

**Test verification** (line 380-391 in phase_test.go):
```go
func TestTransition_rebase_GitCommit_is_noop_for_all_phases(t *testing.T) {
	rebaseCtx := TransitionContext{IsRebaseInProgress: true}
	for _, phase := range allPhases {
		result := Transition(phase, EventGitCommit, rebaseCtx)
		assert.Empty(t, result.Actions, ...)
		assert.Equal(t, phase, result.NewPhase, ...)
	}
}
```

---

### Backward Compatibility

Empty or unknown phase strings default to **PhaseIdle** via `PhaseFromString()` (lines 24-39). This ensures:
- Legacy session state files (pre-phase-tracking) are treated as idle sessions
- EventGitCommit behavior for old sessions matches PhaseIdle rules

Excellent! Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive analysis. Let me compile the findings:

## Complete Analysis: PostCommit Method Code Paths & Phase State Machine

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a comprehensive breakdown:

---

### 1. EVERY CODE PATH THROUGH PostCommit's ACTION DISPATCH LOOP (lines 583-616)

The loop processes each session's state machine actions. There are **5 distinct code paths**:

| Path # | Action Dispatched | Condition | Code Lines | Phase Transition(s) |
|--------|-------------------|-----------|------------|-------------------|
| **1** | `ActionCondense` | Phase=IDLE + GitCommit | 585-589 | IDLE → IDLE (condensed) |
| **2** | `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` | Phase=ENDED + GitCommit + HasFiles + HasNew | 594-602 | ENDED → ENDED |
| **3** | `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` | Phase=ENDED + GitCommit + NoFiles | 603-608 | ENDED → ENDED |
| **4** | `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` | Phase=ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED + GitCommit | 609-612 | ACTIVE→ACTIVE_COMMITTED or ACTIVE_COMMITTED→ACTIVE_COMMITTED |
| **5** | No action or ActionUpdateLastInteraction | (Handled by ApplyCommonActions) | 613-615 | Various (depends on phase + event) |

---

### 2. PHASE + EVENT COMBINATIONS THAT TRIGGER EACH PATH

From `phase.go` transitions for `EventGitCommit`:

```
PhaseIdle + EventGitCommit:
  → NewPhase: IDLE, Actions: [ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
  ➜ Path 1 (if hasNew=true) or bypass condensation (if hasNew=false)

PhaseActive + EventGitCommit:
  → NewPhase: ACTIVE_COMMITTED, Actions: [ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
  ➜ Path 4

PhaseActiveCommitted + EventGitCommit:
  → NewPhase: ACTIVE_COMMITTED, Actions: [ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
  ➜ Path 4

PhaseEnded + EventGitCommit (HasFilesTouched=true):
  → NewPhase: ENDED, Actions: [ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
  ➜ Path 2 (if hasNew=true)

PhaseEnded + EventGitCommit (HasFilesTouched=false):
  → NewPhase: ENDED, Actions: [ActionDiscardIfNoFiles, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
  ➜ Path 3

PhaseIdle/Active/ActiveCommitted/Ended + EventGitCommit (IsRebaseInProgress=true):
  → No transition at all (line 537-541 in manual_commit_hooks.go)
  → Stays current phase, no actions executed
  ➜ Path 5 (early return, no action dispatch)
```

---

### 3. TEST COVERAGE ANALYSIS

**File:** `phase_postcommit_test.go` (existing tests)

| Test Name | Path Covered | Phase Transitions | Coverage Status |
|-----------|--------------|-------------------|-----------------|
| `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation` | Path 4 | ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED | ✓ Covered |
| `TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses` | Path 1 | IDLE → IDLE (condensed) | ✓ Covered |
| `TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition` | Path 5 | ACTIVE (no transition) | ✓ Covered |
| `TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists` | Path 1 + Path 4 | Multi-session scenario | ✓ Covered |

**NOT COVERED:**
- Path 2: `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` with ENDED phase
- Path 3: `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` with ENDED phase
- Condensation **failure** scenarios (any path where `condenseAndUpdateState()` returns false)
- ACTIVE_COMMITTED → ACTIVE_COMMITTED transition (multiple commits without stop)
- Edge cases where `hasNew` check differs from action expectation

---

```

---

### 5. CAN WE INJECT A CONDENSATION FAILURE IN TESTS?

**Short answer:** YES, multiple ways.

**How `CondenseSession` can fail (from `manual_commit_condensation.go` lines 106-150):**

1. **Shadow branch doesn't exist** (line 110-113)
   ```
   if err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
       return nil, fmt.Errorf("shadow branch not found: %w", err)
   }
   ```
   → **Inject by:** Deleting the shadow branch before PostCommit

2. **extractSessionData fails** (line 118-121)
   ```
   if err := s.extractSessionData(...); err != nil {
       return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   }
   ```
   → **Inject by:** Corrupting the transcript file in `.entire/metadata/`

3. **getCheckpointStore fails** (line 124-127)
   ```
   if err := s.getCheckpointStore(); err != nil {
       return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   }
   ```
   → **Inject by:** Making git repo unavailable (unlikely, but repo is passed in)

4. **WriteCommitted fails** (occurs later in CondenseSession, not shown in excerpt)
   → **Inject by:** Making `.entire/sessions` branch unwritable
   
5. **Author info unavailable** (line 130)
   → **Inject by:** Unconfigured git user (but test helper configures this)

**Practical test setup for condensation failure:**

```go
// In a new test:
dir := setupGitRepo(t)
setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)

// Set phase to IDLE to trigger condensation
state, _ := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
s.saveSessionState(state)

// DELETE the shadow branch to trigger CondenseSession error
shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
repo.Storer.RemoveReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch))

// Create commit with trailer
commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")

// PostCommit will fail internally but return nil (see line 685-687)
err := s.PostCommit()
require.NoError(t, err) // Hook must return nil even on failure

// BUT check stderr output shows warning
// (Warning is printed to os.Stderr at line 685-686)
```

---

### 6. SUMMARY TABLE: CODE PATH COVERAGE

| Path | Action | Phase | Trigger | Tests | Status |
|------|--------|-------|---------|-------|--------|
| 1 | ActionCondense | IDLE | GitCommit + hasNew | TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses | ✓ Covered |
| 1 | ActionCondense | IDLE | GitCommit + !hasNew | updateBaseCommitIfChanged | ⚠ Implicit |
| 2 | ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched | ENDED | GitCommit + Files + hasNew | **NONE** | ❌ NOT COVERED |
| 3 | ActionDiscardIfNoFiles | ENDED | GitCommit + !Files | **NONE** | ❌ NOT COVERED |
| 4 | ActionMigrateShadowBranch | ACTIVE | GitCommit | TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation | ✓ Covered |
| 4 | ActionMigrateShadowBranch | ACTIVE_COMMITTED | GitCommit | **NONE** | ❌ NOT COVERED |
| 5 | (Rebase skip) | Any | GitCommit + Rebase | TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition | ✓ Covered |

---

### Key Insights for Test Writing:

1. **Missing ENDED phase tests:** Need tests for paths 2 and 3 (ENDED → ENDED transitions)
2. **Condensation failure:** Tests can inject by removing shadow branch or corrupting transcript
3. **Multi-commit scenarios:** ACTIVE_COMMITTED needs explicit test (second commit without Turn End)
4. **hasNew content check:** Separate from condensation - sessionHasNewContent() (lines 757-871)
5. **Two-pass strategy:** Condensations happen before migrations (lines 550-656) - important for test ordering

Fix: don't update BaseCommit when condensation was attempted but failed.\n\n2. **IDLE + hasNew=false**: ActionCondense fires, but `hasNew` is false → takes `else` branch → only updates BaseCommit (no condensation). ---

## Implementation Plan: PostCommit Action Dispatch Tests and Bug Fix

### 1. Bug Analysis

**Location:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`, lines 581-623

**Root Cause:** The fallthrough guard at line 621 uses `!condensed` to decide whether to call `updateBaseCommitIfChanged`. However, `condensed` only becomes `true` on **successful** condensation. When condensation is **attempted but fails** (e.g., `condenseAndUpdateState` returns `false`), `condensed` remains `false`, triggering the fallthrough. This updates `state.BaseCommit` to the new HEAD, orphaning the shadow branch (which is still named after the old BaseCommit).

**Impact:** After this happens, the session state file says `base_commit = <new HEAD>`, but the shadow branch is named `entire/<old-HEAD[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>`. No shadow branch exists for the new HEAD, so all prior checkpoint data becomes inaccessible. Subsequent `SaveChanges` will create a new, empty shadow branch, losing the pre-existing session data.

**Fix:** Introduce a `condenseAttempted` boolean that is set `true` whenever condensation is attempted (regardless of success/failure). The fallthrough guard should be:
```go
if !condensed && !condenseAttempted && !hasPendingMigration {
    s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
}
```

This is a minimal, targeted fix. When condensation is attempted but fails, `condenseAttempted` is `true`, so `BaseCommit` is NOT updated. The shadow branch naming remains consistent with the session state, and the next commit (or retry) can attempt condensation again from the same shadow branch.

### 2. Test Cases

All tests go in `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`.

#### Test 1: `TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch` (BUG DEMONSTRATION)

**What it verifies:** When condensation fails (shadow branch deleted before PostCommit), `BaseCommit` should NOT be updated to the new HEAD.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseIdle`
2. Record the `BaseCommit` and shadow branch name
3. Delete the shadow branch reference (`repo.Storer.RemoveReference(...)`) to simulate condensation failure
4. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer` to create new HEAD
5. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `state.BaseCommit` should still equal the OLD BaseCommit (not updated to new HEAD)
- `state.StepCount` should NOT be reset (condensation did not succeed)
- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist (no condensation happened)
- `state.Phase` should still be `PhaseIdle` (phase transition still applied, but BaseCommit preserved)

**Why it demonstrates the bug:** Before the fix, this test will FAIL because `BaseCommit` gets updated to the new HEAD due to the fallthrough. After the fix, `condenseAttempted=true` prevents the fallthrough.

#### Test 2: `TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit`

**What it verifies:** When an IDLE session has `hasNew=false` (no new content since last condensation), `ActionCondense` fires but takes the `else` branch, updating `BaseCommit` without attempting condensation.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseIdle`
2. Set `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart` to equal the transcript line count (simulating all content already condensed)
3. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
4. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `state.BaseCommit` equals the new HEAD (updated via the `else` branch)
- `state.StepCount` should NOT be reset (no condensation happened)
- Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up since no condensation)
- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist

#### Test 3: `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses`

**What it verifies:** ENDED phase with `FilesTouched` non-empty and `hasNew=true` triggers `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched`, which condenses and cleans up.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseEnded`
2. Set `state.FilesTouched` to `["test.txt"]`
3. Set `state.EndedAt` to some past time
4. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
5. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `entire/sessions` branch exists (condensation happened)
- Shadow branch is deleted
- `state.BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD
- `state.StepCount` reset to 0
- `state.Phase` remains `PhaseEnded`

#### Test 4: `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent`

**What it verifies:** ENDED phase with `FilesTouched` and `hasNew=false` does NOT condense but DOES update BaseCommit via fallthrough.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseEnded`
2. Set `state.FilesTouched` to `["test.txt"]`
3. Set `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart` to equal the transcript line count
4. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
5. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
- Shadow branch still exists
- `state.BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD (fallthrough applies because nothing was attempted)
- `state.StepCount` unchanged

#### Test 5: `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards`

**What it verifies:** ENDED phase with empty `FilesTouched` triggers `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` and falls through to update BaseCommit.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseEnded`
2. Ensure `state.FilesTouched` is `nil` (or empty)
3. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
4. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
- `state.BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD (fallthrough)
- `state.StepCount` unchanged
- `state.Phase` remains `PhaseEnded`

#### Test 6: `TestPostCommit_ActiveCommittedSession_MigratesShadowBranch`

**What it verifies:** ACTIVE_COMMITTED receiving another `GitCommit` event triggers `ActionMigrateShadowBranch`, migrating the shadow branch and staying in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseActiveCommitted`
2. Record original shadow branch name
3. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
4. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `state.Phase` is `PhaseActiveCommitted`
- `state.BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD
- New shadow branch exists (named after new HEAD)
- Old shadow branch deleted (via migration rename)
- `state.StepCount` unchanged (no condensation)
- `hasPendingMigration` was true, so fallthrough did NOT fire

#### Test 7: `TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch`

**What it verifies:** Same as Test 1, but for ENDED phase with `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched`. Confirms the bug fix also covers this action type.

**Setup:**
1. `setupGitRepo` + `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` with session in `PhaseEnded`
2. Set `state.FilesTouched` to `["test.txt"]`
3. Delete shadow branch reference
4. `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`
5. Call `PostCommit()`

**Assertions:**
- `state.BaseCommit` still equals old BaseCommit (not updated)
- `state.StepCount` unchanged
- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist

### 3. Bug Fix

**File:** `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`

**Change:** In the `PostCommit` method, inside the `for _, state := range sessions` loop:

1. Add a `condenseAttempted` boolean alongside `condensed`:
```go
condensed := false
condenseAttempted := false
hasPendingMigration := false
```

2. Set `condenseAttempted = true` in both condensation branches BEFORE calling `condenseAndUpdateState`:

For `ActionCondense`:
```go
case session.ActionCondense:
    if hasNew {
        condenseAttempted = true
        if s.condenseAndUpdateState(...) {
            condensed = true
        }
    } else {
        s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
    }
```

For `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched`:
```go
case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
    if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {
        condenseAttempted = true
        if s.condenseAndUpdateState(...) {
            condensed = true
        }
    }
```

3. Update the fallthrough guard:
```go
if !condensed && !condenseAttempted && !hasPendingMigration {
    s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
}
```

### 4. Order of Operations

1. **Write all 7 test functions** in `phase_postcommit_test.go`
2. **Run tests:** `mise run test` -- Tests 1 and 7 (condensation failure tests) should FAIL, demonstrating the bug. All other new tests should PASS.
3. **Apply the bug fix** (3 lines changed in `manual_commit_hooks.go`)
4. **Run tests again:** All 7 new tests + existing 4 tests should PASS.
5. **Run full suite:** `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`

### 5. Helper Considerations

The existing test helpers (`setupGitRepo`, `setupSessionWithCheckpoint`, `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`) are sufficient for all 7 tests. No new helpers are needed. The shadow branch deletion for condensation failure injection is a simple 2-line inline operation:

```go
shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
err = repo.Storer.RemoveReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch))
require.NoError(t, err)
```

For the "no new content" scenario (Tests 2 and 4), setting `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart` to a value >= the number of transcript lines in the checkpoint makes `sessionHasNewContent` return `false`. The transcript created by `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` has 2 lines, so setting `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2` works.

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` - Primary file: add 7 new test functions following existing patterns
- `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` - Bug fix location: add `condenseAttempted` boolean to PostCommit's dispatch loop (lines ~581-623)
- `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_wiring_test.go` - Reference for test helpers (`setupGitRepo`, `writeTestFile`)
- `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go` - Reference (read-only): state machine transitions and action types to understand which paths to test
- `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` - Reference (read-only): `CondenseSession` failure path understanding

Good — so for `hasNew=false` via shadow branch path, we need `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart >= transcriptLines`. The transcript from `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` has 2 lines, so setting `CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2` makes `hasNew = false`.

But wait — for the condensation failure test (shadow branch deleted), `sessionHasNewContent` will also fail to find the shadow branch and fall through to `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`. That function needs `TranscriptPath` and `AgentType` — if those are empty (which they are in the test setup), it returns `false, nil`. So with the shadow branch deleted, `hasNew=false` and the code takes the `else` branch of `ActionCondense`, not the condensation path. That changes the test approach.

Let me re-examine. To get condensation to *attempt and fail*, we need `hasNew=true` AND `condenseAndUpdateState` to fail. The shadow branch must exist for `sessionHasNewContent` to return `true`, but then `CondenseSession` must fail. Let me look at what `CondenseSession` does after finding the shadow branch.

Now I have a clear understanding of all the nuances. The key challenge is how to inject a condensation failure: we need `hasNew=true` (so the condensation path is entered) but `CondenseSession` to fail. The approach is to corrupt the shadow branch reference to point to `plumbing.ZeroHash` — `repo.Reference()` succeeds but `repo.CommitObject()` fails inside both `sessionHasNewContent` (triggering fail-open → `hasNew=true`) and `CondenseSession`.

Let me write the plan.

> TOOL

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/.claude/plans/nifty-sniffing-pelican.md",
  "content": "# Plan: PostCommit Action Dispatch Test Coverage + Bug Fix\n\n## Context\n\nThe PostCommit method's action dispatch loop in `manual_commit_hooks.go:580-634` has ~6 branches but only 2-3 are tested. A confirmed bug exists where condensation failure orphans shadow branches by incorrectly updating `BaseCommit`. Before we can simplify the cyclomatic complexity, we need a safety net of comprehensive tests.\n\nCursor[bot] identified the bug on PR #172 — the one unaddressed review comment.\n\n## Bug Summary\n\nWhen `condenseAndUpdateState()` returns `false` (condensation failed), `condensed` stays `false`, so the fallthrough at line 621 calls `updateBaseCommitIfChanged()`. This updates `state.BaseCommit` to the new HEAD, but the shadow branch is still named with the **old** BaseCommit hash. Session data becomes inaccessible.\n\n## Approach: TDD — Tests First, Then Fix\n\n### Step 1: Add 7 test cases to `phase_postcommit_test.go`\n\nAll tests use existing helpers: `setupGitRepo`, `setupSessionWithCheckpoint`, `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`.\n\n| # | Test Name | Phase | Scenario | Key Assertions |\n|---|-----------|-------|----------|----------------|\n| 1 | `TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch` | IDLE | Shadow branch corrupted (ZeroHash) → condensation fails | `BaseCommit` unchanged (old value); `StepCount` not reset; no `entire/sessions` branch |\n| 2 | `TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit` | IDLE | `CheckpointTranscriptStart=2` → `hasNew=false` | `BaseCommit` updated to new HEAD; shadow branch preserved; no condensation |\n| 3 | `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=[\"test.txt\"]`, `hasNew=true` | `entire/sessions` exists; shadow branch deleted; `StepCount` reset |\n| 4 | `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=[\"test.txt\"]`, `CheckpointTranscriptStart=2` | No condensation; `BaseCommit` updated; shadow branch preserved |\n| 5 | `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=nil` | No condensation; `BaseCommit` updated; phase stays ENDED |\n| 6 | `TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch` | ACTIVE_COMMITTED | Second commit while active | Phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED; shadow branch migrated to new HEAD |\n| 7 | `TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=[\"test.txt\"]`, shadow branch corrupted | Same as #1 but for ENDED phase path |\n\n**Condensation failure injection (tests 1 & 7):** Corrupt the shadow branch reference to point at `plumbing.ZeroHash`. All other new tests should pass.\n\n### Step 3: Fix the bug (3 lines changed)\n\n**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nAdd `condenseAttempted` boolean alongside existing `condensed`:\n\n```go\ncondensed := false\ncondenseAttempted := false   // NEW\nhasPendingMigration := false\n```\n\nSet it in both condensation branches:\n\n```go\ncase session.ActionCondense:\n    if hasNew {\n        condenseAttempted = true   // NEW\n        if s.condenseAndUpdateState(...) { condensed = true }\n    } else { ... }\n\ncase session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:\n    if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {\n        condenseAttempted = true   // NEW\n        if s.condenseAndUpdateState(...) { condensed = true }\n    }\n```\n\nUpdate fallthrough guard:\n\n```go\nif !condensed && !condenseAttempted && !hasPendingMigration {   // CHANGED\n    s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)\n}\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run tests — all pass\n\n### Step 5: Reply to PR review comment\n\nReply to cursor[bot]'s thread with the fix commit SHA.\n\n## Files Modified\n\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` — 7 new test functions\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — 3-line bug fix\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n"

> AGENT

Good point. The `condenseAttempted` boolean adds complexity — it's fighting the symptom, not the structure. Let me rethink.

The root issue is the fallthrough pattern: booleans accumulate state across branches, then a conditional at the end has to reason about all of them. Instead, each branch should handle its own `BaseCommit` update — making the fallthrough unnecessary.

> AGENT

Here's the insight: `condensed` and `hasPendingMigration` are only used for the fallthrough guard at line 621. `condenseAndUpdateState` already updates `BaseCommit` on success (line 694 inside that function). So if we make each branch handle its own `BaseCommit` update, we can **remove** both booleans and the fallthrough entirely:

| Branch | Current behavior | Change |
|--------|-----------------|--------|
| `ActionCondense` + `hasNew` | Calls condense, sets `condensed=true` on success | Drop `condensed=true` (condense handles BaseCommit internally) |
| `ActionCondense` + `!hasNew` | Calls `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` | No change |
| `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` + files+hasNew | Calls condense, sets `condensed=true` | Drop `condensed=true` |
| `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` + otherwise | Does nothing → falls through | **Add** `else { updateBaseCommitIfChanged }` |
| `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` | Logs → falls through | **Add** `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` after log |
| `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` | Sets `hasPendingMigration=true` | Drop boolean (migration handles BaseCommit in pass 2) |
| **Fallthrough (lines 618-623)** | Complex conditional | **Remove entirely** |

**Net result:** Remove 2 local variables, remove 1 conditional block, add 2 explicit `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` calls. Each branch is self-contained. Cyclomatic complexity goes **down** while also fixing the bug.

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: PostCommit Action Dispatch Test Coverage + Bug Fix

## Context

The PostCommit method's action dispatch loop in `manual_commit_hooks.go:580-634` has ~6 branches but only 2-3 are tested. A confirmed bug exists where condensation failure orphans shadow branches by incorrectly updating `BaseCommit`. Before we can simplify the cyclomatic complexity, we need a safety net of comprehensive tests.

Cursor[bot] identified the bug on PR #172 — the one unaddressed review comment.

## Bug Summary

When `condenseAndUpdateState()` returns `false` (condensation failed), `condensed` stays `false`, so the fallthrough at line 621 calls `updateBaseCommitIfChanged()`. This updates `state.BaseCommit` to the new HEAD, but the shadow branch is still named with the **old** BaseCommit hash. Session data becomes inaccessible.

## Fix Approach: Eliminate the Fallthrough Pattern

Instead of adding a third boolean (`condenseAttempted`), restructure so each action branch handles its own `BaseCommit` update explicitly. This **removes** the fallthrough and its booleans, reducing cyclomatic complexity while fixing the bug.

**Remove:**
- `condensed` boolean (line 581)
- `hasPendingMigration` boolean (line 582)
- Fallthrough block (lines 618-623)

**Add:**
- `else { updateBaseCommitIfChanged }` to `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` branch
- `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` to `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` branch

Each branch becomes self-contained:

| Action | Condition | BaseCommit handling |
|--------|-----------|-------------------|
| `ActionCondense` | `hasNew=true`, success | `condenseAndUpdateState` handles it |
| `ActionCondense` | `hasNew=true`, failure | **Don't update** (preserves shadow branch) |
| `ActionCondense` | `hasNew=false` | Explicit `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` (already exists) |
| `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` | files+hasNew, success | `condenseAndUpdateState` handles it |
| `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` | files+hasNew, failure | **Don't update** (preserves shadow branch) |
| `ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched` | otherwise | Explicit `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` **(new)** |
| `ActionDiscardIfNoFiles` | any | Explicit `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` **(new)** |
| `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` | any | Migration handles in pass 2 |

## Step-by-Step

### Step 1: Add 7 test cases to `phase_postcommit_test.go`

All tests use existing helpers: `setupGitRepo`, `setupSessionWithCheckpoint`, `commitWithCheckpointTrailer`.

| # | Test Name | Phase | Scenario | Key Assertions |
|---|-----------|-------|----------|----------------|
| 1 | `CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch` | IDLE | Shadow branch corrupted (ZeroHash) → condensation fails | `BaseCommit` unchanged; `StepCount` not reset; no `entire/sessions` |
| 2 | `IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit` | IDLE | `CheckpointTranscriptStart=2` → `hasNew=false` | `BaseCommit` updated; shadow branch preserved; no condensation |
| 3 | `EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=["test.txt"]`, `hasNew=true` | `entire/sessions` exists; shadow branch deleted; `StepCount` reset |
| 4 | `EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=["test.txt"]`, `CheckpointTranscriptStart=2` | No condensation; `BaseCommit` updated; shadow branch preserved |
| 5 | `EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=nil` | No condensation; `BaseCommit` updated; phase stays ENDED |
| 6 | `ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch` | ACTIVE_COMMITTED | Second commit while active | Phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED; shadow branch migrated |
| 7 | `CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch` | ENDED | `FilesTouched=["test.txt"]`, shadow branch corrupted | Same as #1 for ENDED path |

**Condensation failure injection (tests 1 & 7):** Overwrite the shadow branch ref to point at `plumbing.ZeroHash`:
- `sessionHasNewContent` → error → fail-open `hasNew=true`
- `CondenseSession` → `CommitObject(ZeroHash)` fails → `condenseAndUpdateState` returns `false`

**No-new-content injection (tests 2 & 4):** Set `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2` (transcript from `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` has exactly 2 lines).

### Step 2: Run tests — verify bug tests (1 & 7) fail

### Step 3: Apply the fix to `manual_commit_hooks.go`

### Step 4: Run tests — all pass

### Step 5: `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`

### Step 6: Reply to cursor[bot]'s PR review comment with fix commit SHA

## Files Modified

- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` — 7 new test functions
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — restructure action dispatch (net reduction in complexity)

   197→	idleState.LastInteractionTime = nil
   198→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(idleState))
   199→
   200→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent session)
   201→	// Save the active session with ACTIVE phase and some checkpoints

   207→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   208→		StartedAt:           now,
   209→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   210→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   211→		StepCount:           1,
   212→	}
   213→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(activeState))
   214→
   215→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   216→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   217→

   219→	err = s.PostCommit()
   220→	require.NoError(t, err)
   221→
   222→	// Verify the ACTIVE session's phase is now ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   223→	activeState, err = s.loadSessionState(activeSessionID)
   224→	require.NoError(t, err)
   225→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, activeState.Phase,
   226→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
   227→
   228→	// Verify the IDLE session actually condensed (entire/sessions branch should exist)
   229→	idleState, err = s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   230→	require.NoError(t, err)
   231→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   232→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after IDLE session condensation")
   233→	require.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   234→
   235→	// Verify IDLE session's StepCount was reset by condensation
   236→	assert.Equal(t, 0, idleState.StepCount,
   237→		"IDLE session StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   238→
   239→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted because the ACTIVE session still needs it.
   240→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD via migration.
   241→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(activeState.BaseCommit, activeState.WorktreeID)
   242→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   243→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   244→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   245→		"shadow branch should be preserved when an active session still exists on it")
   246→}

   248→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
   249→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
   250→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
   251→	t.Helper()
   252→
   253→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
   254→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   255→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   256→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   257→
   258→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
   259→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
   260→`
   261→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(

   280→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
   281→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
   282→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
   283→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
   284→	t.Helper()
   285→
   286→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   287→
   288→	// Modify a file so there is something to commit
   289→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
   290→	content := "updated at " + time.Now().String()
   291→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
   292→
   293→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   294→	require.NoError(t, err)
   295→
   296→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
   297→	require.NoError(t, err)
   298→
   299→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
   300→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   301→		Author: &object.Signature{
   302→			Name:  "Test",
   303→			Email: "test@test.com",
   304→			When:  time.Now(),
   305→		},
   306→	})
   307→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
   308→}

Corrupt shadow branch: `repo.Storer.SetReference(plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash))`\n5. commitWithCheckpointTrailer\n6. PostCommit()\n\nAssertions:\n- No error from PostCommit (hooks fail gracefully)\n- `state.BaseCommit` == original BaseCommit (NOT updated to new HEAD)\n- `state.StepCount` == original StepCount (not reset)\n- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist\n- `state.Phase` == PhaseIdle (phase transition still applies)\n\n### Test 2: `TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit`\nIDLE session, no new content since last condensation → doesn't condense, just updates BaseCommit.\n\nSetup:\n1. setupGitRepo + setupSessionWithCheckpoint\n2. Set phase to IDLE, set `state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2` (transcript has exactly 2 lines)\n3. Record shadow branch name\n4. commitWithCheckpointTrailer\n5. Get new HEAD hash for comparison: `head, _ := repo.Head()` after commit\n6. PostCommit()\n\nAssertions:\n- `state.BaseCommit` == new HEAD hash (updated)\n- Shadow branch still exists (not deleted)\n- `entire/sessions` branch should NOT exist (no condensation)\n- `state.StepCount` unchanged\n\n### Test 3: `TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses`\nENDED session with files touched and new content → condenses.\n\nSetup:\n1. setupGitRepo + setupSessionWithCheckpoint\n2.     22→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    23→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.

   248→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
   249→// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
   250→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   251→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   252→	t.Chdir(dir)
   253→
   254→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)

   267→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil

   269→
   270→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
   271→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   272→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount

   296→	assert.Error(t, err,

   298→

   392→

   394→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
   395→
   396→	// Run PostCommit
   397→	err = s.PostCommit()
   398→	require.NoError(t, err)
   399→
   400→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists (condensation happened)
   401→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   402→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   403→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   404→
   405→	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
   406→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   407→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   408→	assert.Error(t, err,
   409→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
   410→
   411→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   412→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   413→	require.NoError(t, err)
   414→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   415→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")

   417→	// Phase stays ENDED

   421→
   422→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
   423→// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
   424→// and updates BaseCommit via fallthrough.
   425→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent(t *testing.T) {
   426→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   427→	t.Chdir(dir)
   428→
   429→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   430→	require.NoError(t, err)
   431→
   432→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   433→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-no-content"
   434→
   435→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   436→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   437→
   438→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched but no new content
   439→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   440→	require.NoError(t, err)
   441→	now := time.Now()
   442→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   443→	state.EndedAt = &now
   444→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   445→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   446→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   447→
   448→	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
   449→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   450→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   451→
   452→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   453→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
   454→
   455→	// Get new HEAD hash
   456→	head, err := repo.Head()
   457→	require.NoError(t, err)
   458→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   459→
   460→	// Run PostCommit
   461→	err = s.PostCommit()
   462→	require.NoError(t, err)
   463→
   464→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
   465→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   466→	assert.Error(t, err,
   467→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no new content")
   468→
   469→	// Shadow branch should still exist
   470→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   471→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   472→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   473→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   474→
   475→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
   476→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   477→	require.NoError(t, err)
   478→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   479→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
   480→
   481→	// StepCount unchanged
   482→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   483→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   484→}
   485→
   486→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED
   487→// session with no files touched takes the discard path, updating BaseCommit.
   488→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards(t *testing.T) {
   489→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   490→	t.Chdir(dir)
   491→
   492→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   493→	require.NoError(t, err)
   494→
   495→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   496→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-discard"
   497→
   498→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   499→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   500→
   501→	// Set phase to ENDED with no files touched
   502→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   503→	require.NoError(t, err)
   504→	now := time.Now()
   505→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   506→	state.EndedAt = &now
   507→	state.FilesTouched = nil // No files touched
   508→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   509→
   510→	// Record original StepCount
   511→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   512→
   513→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   514→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b3")
   515→
   516→	// Get new HEAD hash
   517→	head, err := repo.Head()
   518→	require.NoError(t, err)
   519→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   520→
   521→	// Run PostCommit
   522→	err = s.PostCommit()
   523→	require.NoError(t, err)
   524→
   525→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation for discard path)
   526→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   527→	assert.Error(t, err,
   528→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist for discard path")
   529→
   530→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
   531→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   532→	require.NoError(t, err)
   533→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   534→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD on discard path")
   535→
   536→	// StepCount unchanged
   537→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   538→		"StepCount should be unchanged on discard path")
   539→
   540→	// Phase stays ENDED
   541→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   542→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED on discard path")
   543→}
   544→
   545→// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
   546→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
   547→// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   548→func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   549→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   550→	t.Chdir(dir)
   551→
   552→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   553→	require.NoError(t, err)
   554→
   555→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   556→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
   557→
   558→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   559→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   560→
   561→	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   562→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   563→	require.NoError(t, err)
   564→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   565→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   566→
   567→	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
   568→	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   569→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   570→
   571→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   572→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
   573→
   574→	// Run PostCommit
   575→	err = s.PostCommit()
   576→	require.NoError(t, err)
   577→
   578→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   579→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   580→	require.NoError(t, err)
   581→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
   582→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
   583→
   584→	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
   585→	head, err := repo.Head()
   586→	require.NoError(t, err)
   587→	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
   588→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
   589→
   590→	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
   591→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   592→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   593→	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
   594→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   595→		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
   596→
   597→	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
   598→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
   599→	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
   600→	assert.Error(t, err,
   601→		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
   602→
   603→	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
   604→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   605→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   606→
   607→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   608→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   609→	assert.Error(t, err,
   610→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   611→}
   612→
   613→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
   614→// that when condensation fails for an ENDED session with files touched,
   615→// BaseCommit is preserved (not updated).
   616→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   617→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   618→	t.Chdir(dir)
   619→
   620→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   621→	require.NoError(t, err)
   622→
   623→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   624→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-ended"
   625→
   626→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   627→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   628→
   629→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   630→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   631→	require.NoError(t, err)
   632→	now := time.Now()
   633→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   634→	state.EndedAt = &now
   635→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   636→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   637→
   638→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount
   639→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   640→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   641→
   642→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   643→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   644→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   645→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   646→
   647→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   648→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d5")
   649→
   650→	// Run PostCommit
   651→	err = s.PostCommit()
   652→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   653→
   654→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   655→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   656→	require.NoError(t, err)
   657→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   658→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   659→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   660→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails for ENDED session")
   661→
   662→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   663→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   664→	assert.Error(t, err,
   665→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   666→
   667→	// Phase stays ENDED
   668→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   669→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED when condensation fails")
   670→}
   671→
   672→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
   673→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
   674→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
   675→	t.Helper()
   676→
   677→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
   678→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   679→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   680→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   681→
   682→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
   683→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
   684→`
   685→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
   686→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   687→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
   688→
   689→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
   690→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   691→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   692→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   693→		NewFiles:       []string{},
   694→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   695→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   696→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   697→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
   698→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   699→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   700→	})
   701→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
   702→}
   703→
   704→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
   705→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
   706→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
   707→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
   708→	t.Helper()
   709→
   710→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   711→
   712→	// Modify a file so there is something to commit
   713→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
   714→	content := "updated at " + time.Now().String()
   715→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
   716→
   717→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   718→	require.NoError(t, err)
   719→
   720→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
   721→	require.NoError(t, err)
   722→
   723→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
   724→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   725→		Author: &object.Signature{
   726→			Name:  "Test",
   727→			Email: "test@test.com",
   728→			When:  time.Now(),
   729→		},
   730→	})
   731→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
   732→}
   733→

index 803ee2a4..3dd78b0a 100644

+// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
+// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
+func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-idle"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to IDLE
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
+	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
+	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
+	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
+
+	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
+
+	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
+
+	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
+		"phase should remain IDLE when condensation fails")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when
+// an IDLE session has no new transcript content since last condensation,
+// PostCommit skips condensation but still updates BaseCommit.
+func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-no-content"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to IDLE with CheckpointTranscriptStart matching transcript length (2 lines)
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
+	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
+	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record shadow branch name
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "f6a1b2c3d4e5")
+
+	// Get new HEAD hash for comparison
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD when no new content")
+
+	// Shadow branch should still exist (not deleted, no condensation)
+	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
+	require.NoError(t, err,
+		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
+
+	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no condensation happened")
+
+	// StepCount should be unchanged
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
+// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
+func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-condenses"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	now := time.Now()
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	state.EndedAt = &now
+	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record shadow branch name
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists (condensation happened)
+	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
+	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
+
+	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
+	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
+
+	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
+
+	// Phase stays ENDED
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
+		"ENDED session should stay ENDED after condensation")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
+// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
+// and updates BaseCommit via fallthrough.
+func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-no-content"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched but no new content
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	now := time.Now()
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	state.EndedAt = &now
+	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
+	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
+
+	// Get new HEAD hash
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no new content")
+
+	// Shadow branch should still exist
+	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
+	require.NoError(t, err,
+		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
+
+	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
+
+	// StepCount unchanged
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED
+// session with no files touched takes the discard path, updating BaseCommit.
+func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-discard"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ENDED with no files touched
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	now := time.Now()
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	state.EndedAt = &now
+	state.FilesTouched = nil // No files touched
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record original StepCount
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b3")
+
+	// Get new HEAD hash
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation for discard path)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist for discard path")
+
+	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD on discard path")
+
+	// StepCount unchanged
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be unchanged on discard path")
+
+	// Phase stays ENDED
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
+		"ENDED session should stay ENDED on discard path")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
+// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
+// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
+func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
+	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
+		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
+
+	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
+
+	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
+	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
+	require.NoError(t, err,
+		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
+
+	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
+	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
+
+	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
+
+	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
+}
+
+// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
+// that when condensation fails for an ENDED session with files touched,
+// BaseCommit is preserved (not updated).
+func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_EndedSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-ended"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	now := time.Now()
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
+	state.EndedAt = &now
+	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount
+	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
+	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
+
+	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d5")
+
+	// Run PostCommit
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
+
+	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails for ENDED session")
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails for ENDED session")
+
+	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
+
+	// Phase stays ENDED
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
+		"ENDED session should stay ENDED when condensation fails")
+}
+
 // setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
 // on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
 func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary:

**Commit:** `eafc9512` (pushed)

**Bug fix** (`manual_commit_hooks.go`):
- Eliminated the fallthrough pattern — each action branch now handles its own `BaseCommit` update
- Removed `condensed` and `hasPendingMigration` booleans and the fallthrough conditional block
- On condensation failure, `BaseCommit` is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible
- Added `logging.Warn` for condensation failures (was only printing to stderr before)
- Changed `condenseAndUpdateState` return type from `bool` to void (return value no longer needed)

**Test coverage** (`phase_postcommit_test.go`): 7 new tests (4 existing → 11 total):
1. Condensation failure preserves shadow branch (IDLE) 
2. No new content updates BaseCommit (IDLE)
3. ENDED + files touched condenses
4. ENDED + files touched, no new content
5. ENDED + no files touched discards
6. ACTIVE_COMMITTED migration
7. Condensation failure preserves shadow branch (ENDED)

**PR review:** Replied to cursor[bot]'s thread with fix details.

> AGENT

Cursor[bot] is correct — `ActionWarnStaleSession` is passed through by `ApplyCommonActions` as a strategy-specific action (line 311), but our switch statement groups it with the "handled above" comment. The comment is misleading.

However, the observation that `EventGitCommit` never produces `ActionWarnStaleSession` is also correct — it's only produced by `EventSessionStart`. So this is purely a comment accuracy issue, not a functional bug. Let me check which events produce it to be sure.

> AGENT

Confirmed: `ActionWarnStaleSession` is only produced by `EventSessionStart` (for PhaseActive and PhaseActiveCommitted), never by `EventGitCommit`. So it can never appear in PostCommit's `remaining` slice. The comment is technically misleading but harmless.

Let me fix the comment to be accurate — it should say these actions are passed through but are not produced by `EventGitCommit`, rather than claiming they're "handled above".
